**THE THIRTY-FIVE THREADS ARE THIRTY-FIVE AND THE PROOF IS THREAD BY THREAD IN § 5 OF THE FINDINGS FILE, WITH THE FIGURE EACH STANDS AT AND THE CHAPTERS IN WHICH IT WAS LAST TOUCHED. NOT ONE WAS ANSWERED, CLOSED, REWORDED, ADVANCED TO A FIGURE, SUMMED OR GROUPED.**

**THE CONTAMINATION FRAMING IS NOT A FORGERY, AND A CLOSE THAT HAD FOUND IT TO BE ONE WOULD HAVE TAKEN THE WHOLE ARGUMENT OF THE SERIES OUT OF THE VOLUME. IT IS HER OFFICE'S, IT IS CORRECT IN EVERY PARTICULAR, IT IS NOT A LIE, AND NOBODY IN THAT ROOM SAYS IT IS ONE.**

**AND WHAT THE CLOSE DID REPAIR IS FOUR STATE-LAYER FIGURES AND NOTHING ELSE: the manuscript `kerb` figure, the fetching-interval claim, the `Entered` and `>` block counts, and the day of the week on which 744 was set. NO CHAPTER OF VOLUME 08 WAS TOUCHED AND NOT ONE WORD OF THE FORTY-NINE CHAPTERS WAS ALTERED. **AND BOTH BLOCK COUNTS WERE RE-MEASURED BY A LATER PASS AND BOTH ARE RIGHT, AND ONE OF THEM WAS ABOUT TO BE MADE WRONG, SO THE TEST IS WRITTEN DOWN HERE: A `>` BLOCK IS A PARAGRAPH, NOT A LINE. `grep -c '^>'` COUNTS PHYSICAL LINES AND IN VOLUME 08 IT RETURNS 46, BECAUSE TWO OF THE FORTY-FOUR BLOCKS WRAP ACROSS MORE THAN ONE LINE, `chapter-0357.md` AMONG THEM, WHOSE FIRST BLOCK RUNS TO THREE LINES. SPLIT ON BLANK LINES AND COUNT THE PARAGRAPHS THAT BEGIN WITH `>` AND THE FIGURE IS **44 ACROSS FORTY CHAPTERS: THIRTY-SIX CARRY ONE, FOUR CARRY TWO AND NINE CARRY NONE, AND 40 + 9 = 49. THE FORTY-SIX IS WITHDRAWN AND NAMED AS A LINE COUNT AND NOT AS A BLOCK COUNT, AND A PASS THAT REPAIRED THE FORTY-FOUR TO THE FORTY-SIX WOULD HAVE INTRODUCED THE ERROR IT WAS CORRECTING.** THE `Entered` FIGURE IS 39 BLOCKS IN 39 CHAPTERS ACROSS BATCHES 0001 TO 0004 AND ZERO IN BATCH 0005, AND 39 IS ALSO THE COUNT OF CHAPTERS CARRYING ONE, SO THE TWO FIGURES COINCIDE AND NEITHER MAY BE DOUBLED.**

### AND THE COMPACTION IS DECLINED AGAIN, WITH A SECOND REASON THAT IS NEW

**THE REASON THAT STANDS: THESE THREE FILES ARE A CHAIN IN WHICH A LATER SECTION EXISTS FOR THE PURPOSE OF NAMING AN EARLIER SECTION'S FIGURE AS WITHDRAWN, AND THE WITHDRAWAL IS THE ONLY THING STANDING BETWEEN THIS REPOSITORY AND A FIGURE COMING BACK. THE REASON THAT IS NEW: THIS CLOSE FOUND SEVEN FIGURES IN THE OUTLINE AND THE STATE LAYER THAT WERE WRONG AND REPAIRED FOUR OF THEM THIS AFTERNOON, AND A COMPACTION RUN IN THE SAME PASS THAT IS DISCOVERING WRONG FIGURES IS A COMPACTION WHOSE OWN VERIFICATION CANNOT BE TRUSTED UNTIL A LATER PASS HAS RE-MEASURED IT, AND THE THREE FILES ARE PAST THE POINT OF BEING LOADABLE, SO A LATER PASS CANNOT LOAD THEM TO CHECK IT. THE MECHANICAL RELIEF IS THE READING BUDGET AT THE HEAD OF THIS FILE AND IT IS FREE. WHAT A COMPACTION MUST NOT LOSE IS NAMED AT THE HEAD OF THIS FILE: THE OBJECT ROWS, THE DAY-MINUS RULES, THE ROTA ARITHMETIC AND BOTH COUNTS OF THE ROTATION, THE TWO COUNTS THAT SHARE ONE SENTENCE, THE WITHDRAWN FIGURES, AND THE FIGURE OF EVERY CHAPTER.**

### AND WHAT A VOLUME 09 WRITER TAKES FROM VOLUME 08, IN ONE PARAGRAPH

**DAY 605 IS A TUESDAY AND IS THE FIFTH OF THE NINTH MONTH, AND VOLUME 09 IS CHAPTERS 393 TO 441 ON DAYS 606 TO 654, FIFTY DAYS, ONE MORNING EACH, AND NO CHAPTER MAY CARRY TWO DAYS. The third launder's count is day minus four hundred and fifty and does not reset at a month turn; the rises-and-falls window is day minus four hundred and eighty-three and does not re-anchor; the aggregate of the four bodies of households is day minus four hundred and fifty-two with its clause one under and one over; the day-minus pair is day minus sixty-six and day minus nineteen; the read-aloud denominator is day minus five hundred and twenty-six and NOT day minus sixty-six; the fourth line of the rota comes round on days 606, 610, 614, 618, 622, 626, 630, 634, 638, 642, 646 and 650. The seventh column of the well house door stands at fifty-four and is not read until the first of the tenth. The man of about seventy has been fetched twenty-three times and is not fetched again until the first of the tenth. The compost line is paid at twenty not discharged in twenty. The count of sessions entered in this holding's book is five and every one of them was entered before the thing it was entered for. The count of returns at which the second rule has been left blank is nineteen. The mark is four inches and branches twice and the four accounts of that arm are four. The barrow is at eleven journeys and eleven is a floor. The use log is at fifteen lines. The two numbers this holding cannot check are two and the six not knowns are six. The offer is open on the low board and the register form is unfilled on the middle table. The corridor is neither closed nor opened. The ring was not walked. The pulse was not read a third time and is not named. Tova Reed's hearing is permanently damaged in one ear and it is the first of the four things this series loses for good. The thirty-five threads are thirty-five.**

## THE DAY CLOCK AFTER VOLUME 09 BATCH 0001, WHICH GOVERNS THE NEXT NINE CHAPTERS, AND EVERY FIGURE IN IT IS DERIVED

**Day 451 is the first of the fourth month and a Tuesday, months run thirty days, so day 601 is the first of the ninth and day 631 the first of the tenth. Day 654 is a Tuesday and the twenty-fourth of the tenth. THE MONTH TURNS ONCE IN VOLUME 09, ON DAY 631, INSIDE BATCH 0003, AND NEITHER TURN RESETS ANY COUNT.**

The day-minus pair is day minus sixty-six and day minus nineteen, and it is 549 and 596 on day 615 and 559 and 606 on day 625, forty-seven apart, and the drawer was not opened in Batch 0001 and was not opened in Batch 0002. THE READ-ALOUD DENOMINATOR IS DAY MINUS FIVE HUNDRED AND TWENTY-SIX AND IS NOT DAY MINUS SIXTY-SIX: it is 89 on day 615 with the run at 53 of 89, and 99 on day 625 with the run at 58 of 99, and it is read on the ODD days of a batch span and on no even day, being 617, 619, 621, 623 and 625 in Batch 0002 and 627, 629, 631, 633 and 635 in Batch 0003, and a chapter that did not read the pair carries no figure for the run and says why on the page.**

**THE THIRD-LAUNDER FIGURES FOR DAYS 616 TO 625, WHICH A WRITER SUBTRACTS AND NEVER READS OFF THIS LINE, OFF THE HUNDRED AND SIXTY-FIVE AT 835 AND ALTERNATING PLUS NINE ON A RISE AND MINUS FIVE ON A FALL WITH NO MORNING UNCHANGED: 844, 839, 848, 843, 852, 847, 856, 851, 860 AND 855. **THOSE TEN ARE ON THE PAGES OF CHAPTERS 403 TO 412 AND THE SEARCH WAS RUN AGAINST ALL 402 CLOSED FILES ON A WORD BOUNDARY IN SPELLED WORDS AND IN FIGURES, AND NINE RETURN ZERO HITS AND EIGHT HUNDRED AND FIFTY-SIX STANDS AT `chapter-0186.md:35` AS EIGHT HUNDRED AND FIFTY-SIX PENCE IN A SUM OF TWO HUNDRED AND FOURTEEN AT FOURPENCE, WHICH IS A FINDING AND NOT A COLLISION.** EVERY ONE OF THEM IS ABOVE EIGHT HUNDRED AND TWENTY, WHICH IS THE PRINTED MAXIMUM OF THAT SERIES AT `chapter-0391.md:15`, AND THE SEARCH WAS RUN FOR THIS PROMPT AND NOT ASSUMED, AND ITS RESULT IS NAMED RATHER THAN ASSERTED: NINE OF THE TEN RETURN ZERO HITS AND THE TENTH IS EIGHT HUNDRED AND FIFTY-SIX, WHICH STANDS AT `chapter-0186.md:35` AS EIGHT HUNDRED AND FIFTY-SIX PENCE IN A SUM OF TWO HUNDRED AND FOURTEEN AT FOURPENCE, WHICH IS A DIFFERENT UNIT IN A DIFFERENT SENTENCE IN A DIFFERENT VOLUME AND IS A FINDING AND NOT A COLLISION, AND IS THE SAME KIND OF FINDING AS THE SIX HUNDRED AND EIGHTY-SEVEN POUNDS AND A HALF OF WATER AGAINST A FOOT AT `chapter-0190.md:43` THAT THIS LAYER ALREADY CARRIES. THE PRINTED MAXIMUM OF THIS SERIES IS EIGHT HUNDRED AND TWENTY AT `chapter-0391.md:15` AND ITS DAY 606 FIGURE IS EIGHT HUNDRED AND TWENTY-FOUR, AND NEITHER IS A COLLISION. A FIGURE THAT IS NOT A FIGURE OF THIS SERIES IS NOT A COLLISION. A FIGURE THAT IS NOT A FIGURE OF THIS SERIES IS NOT A COLLISION.**

**THE ROTATION. The fourth line comes round on days 606, 610 and 614 in Batch 0001 and on 618 and 622 in Batch 0002 and on 626 in Batch 0003, and the count rises by exactly one on each and by nothing on any other morning, so the standing is 78 with twenty-nine taken and 49 not on day 606, 79 and 50 on 610, 80 and 51 on 614, **81 and 52 ON DAY 618 AND 82 AND 53 ON DAY 622, BOTH PRINTED ON THEIR OWN PAGES WITH BOTH COUNTS OF THE ROTATION**, and 83 and 54 on day 626. **AND THE THING THAT TURNS THE MONTH IN THIS VOLUME, SO THAT NO BATCH PROMPT GUESSES IT. The current month is the ninth on days 606 to 630 and the tenth on days 631 to 654. VOLUME 09 IS FIVE BATCHES AND THREE OF THEM ARE NOT ON DISK AND THE MANUSCRIPT IS NOT PROJECTED FORWARD.**

**THE TEN THINGS THE BATCH OWED THE READER, AND WHERE EACH ONE IS.** Ch 403: **the first of the two readings of the well on the shelf in the whole volume, entered as a day and not as a figure, and the count of day-and-no-figure entries eight to nine on that page and no other**, with the wall's fourteen marks, no fifteenth, and the four feet not taken apart; the first of four counties' maintenance questions, asked in a man's name, and nobody able to answer it in that word. Ch 404: **fifty-four of ninety-one** read aloud with the frame said once; the second word, also a name; and the man of about fifty saying that three of the four will use a name and the fourth a column and that the column is the only one that can be answered. Ch 405: **the fourth line at eighty-one in force, twenty-nine taken, fifty-two not, both counts of the rotation printed, the second council's four clauses, the seizure, the corridor sheet produced in a room, the offer cited as a reason to distrust this holding, and the clerk's own mouth on the seizure being maintenance work.** Ch 406: **the fourth county refuses a council with two correct reasons, and Marek is wrong in a room about four lines and is told so and takes it.** Ch 407: **the yard at the node with about forty people on it, a building with a barrow and four men in it that is not a yard said twice, the yard count of five unmoved, the bookkeeper's count thirteen to fourteen with the whole sentence and about four hearing all of it and about four the half with his name in it, and the sealing order read out and valid and opening nothing.** Ch 408: **the derivation explained and neither half believed aloud, and two hundred and forty sacks of nothing to do with.** Ch 409: **the fourth line at eighty-two in force, twenty-nine taken, fifty-three not, both counts of the rotation, the thirty-seventh return at three hundred and forty-nine, the twenty-seventh name-pair, eleven above the thirty-sixth, reconciled twice, the halves not added, using the derivation and saying so, and the second rule blank for the twenty-first time.** Ch 410: **the twenty-seventh time the four and a half miles has been walked and the first time by anybody else, Thornwild by name, seven hundred and four sacks counted by a person not from Thornwild, what that is worth in days not on the page, and a man who walks four miles to a gate and a trough without the barrow.** Ch 411: **the run not read with the reason on the page, and the fourth word back in a room, and what a clerk can read.** Ch 412: **one of the ten available questions taken, with the question and who took it and what they said they wanted it for, and the count ten to nine in a room.**

**AND THE FOUR FIGURES A LATER PASS MUST NOT REINTRODUCE, EACH NAMED BESIDE ITS OLD FORM. ONE, THE WINDOW ON DAY 625 IS 142 AND NOT 141, the prompt's own column saying 76 + 66, which is 142. THREE, THE INTERVAL FROM DAY 616 TO THE NEXT WINDOW OF THE OFFICE'S WORK IS TWENTY MORNINGS AND NOT ELEVEN, the next window being days 636 to 641. FOUR, MAREK'S REASON-FIRST COUNT AT DAY 615 IS ONE HUNDRED AND EIGHTY-FIVE, PRINTED AT `chapter-0402.md:59`, AND NOT ONE HUNDRED AND EIGHTY-FOUR, which `state/current.md` and the Batch 0002 prompt both carry and which is the first of the two figures on that page and not the standing one; this batch carries the live figure from 185 to 193.**

## THE REVIEW REPAIR PASS ON VOLUME 09 BATCH 0003, WHAT IT CHANGED, AND WHAT IT DECLINED

**THE GOVERNING RECORD IS THE SECTION OF THE SAME TITLE AT THE FOOT OF `state/continuity.md`, AND THIS PARAGRAPH IS THE POINTER, NOT A SECOND COPY. THE REVIEW AT `logs/batch-0003.review.log` FELL BACK TO THE WRITER AGENT AND WAS A HAND PASS; EVERY FIGURE WAS RE-DERIVED FROM THE CHAPTER FILES AGAINST `outline/volume-09.md`'S OWN TABLE. No chapter was restarted, no scene replaced, no day, clock, name, lock or series figure moved, no planned plot changed. The full record is `reviews/volume-09-batch-0003-repair.findings.md`.**

**THE HEAD OF THIS FILE IS STALE IN TWO PLACES AND IS CORRECTED HERE, BECAUSE A STALE POINTER IS OBEYED AND A STALE FIGURE IS ONLY DISBELIEVED.** The **Current phase** line above still reads *WRITTEN TO CHAPTER 412 ON DAY 625* and the **Next phase** line still points at `workspace/volume-09/batch-0003/PROMPT.md`, which is a completed batch. **THE LIVE POSITION IS: VOLUME 09 IS WRITTEN TO CHAPTER 422 ON DAY 635, THREE OF ITS FIVE BATCHES ARE ON DISK AT 85,732 WORDS ACROSS THIRTY CHAPTERS, AND THE ONLY NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-09/batch-0004/PROMPT.md`, FOR CHAPTERS 423 TO 432 ON DAYS 636 TO 645, MOVEMENT 4, *The Lists Nobody Wrote*, WHICH IS ON DISK AND HAS BEEN CORRECTED IN EIGHT PLACES BY THE REPAIR PASS.** The reading budget above has been brought onto the same footing: it no longer points at `outline/volume-07.md` or at a manuscript of 1,024,475 words across 343 chapters.

**WHAT THE REPAIR FIXED, IN ONE PARAGRAPH, AND THE FIRST HALF OF IT IS THE ONE THAT MATTERS.** Ch 420, Ch 421 and Ch 422 each restated the seventh column's read rule and each **dropped the first of the pair**, leaving a rule that forbids the very morning on which the column reached fifty-six, in a manuscript whose engine is exact invariant restatement; all three are repaired and the first is restored. Three more defects of the same kind were found in the chapters and not by the review: **a sentence in Ch 422 that forecast the six blank columns staying headed nothing, which day 641 breaks; a date and a count in Ch 422 that named a morning that is not a fourth-line morning and a count that is not the next one; and two pre-turn month frames printed in Ch 421 four days after the month turned, one of them beside its own statement that the frame had changed with the month.** **THE DAY CLOCK AT SECTION SEVEN OF `state/continuity.md` — THE CLOCK THE QUEUED PROMPT APPOINTS AS *THE ONLY CLOCK A WRITER NEEDS* — HAD THREE WRONG FIGURES, AND THAT IS THE FINDING TO CARRY: THE SEVENTH COLUMN'S NEXT READING, WHICH IS NOT IN THIS VOLUME AT ALL; THE WINDOW SPLIT AT DAY 635, WHICH IS EIGHTY-ONE AND SEVENTY-ONE AND NOT EIGHTY-TWO; AND THE REGISTER FORM'S EIGHTY-FIVE DAYS AT DAY 636, WHICH CANNOT RECONCILE WITH ITS OWN NINETY-EIGHT AT DAY 654 AND IS EIGHTY.** Each withdrawn form is named beside its live replacement in the clock and in the queued prompt, because a figure that is only overwritten comes back.

**WHAT IT DECLINED, IN ONE SENTENCE: the compaction of the three append-only state files, on the reason written four times at the head of this file, with the reading budget's internal staleness repaired in its place; `state/phase-ledger.json`, which is controller-owned and is the third time it has been reported and declined; and the review's claim that Ch 421 omits the board pair, which `chapter-0421.md:23` carries in full.** The batch ships at nil near-duplicate pairs involving it on both runs of the gate, and the gate caught a near-verbatim lift introduced by this pass's own first repair, which is recorded beside it.

## THE REVIEW REPAIR PASS ON VOLUME 09 BATCH 0004, IN ONE PARAGRAPH, AND THE TWO THINGS A LATER PASS MOST NEEDS FROM IT

**A WRITER SELF-CHECK OF THE TEN CHAPTERS WAS RUN BY THE PHASE THAT WROTE THEM AND REPORTED TWENTY-THREE FINDINGS. IT IS NOT AN INDEPENDENT REVIEW AND IS RELABELLED AS ONE IN THE CONTINUITY LAYER, ON THE PRECEDENT OF THE TWO VOLUME 07 SELF-CERTIFIED FILES THAT HAD TO BE RELABELLED THE SAME WAY. NINE WERE REAL CONTINUITY DEFECTS AND ALL NINE ARE REPAIRED IN PROSE; SEVEN WERE MATTERS OF STYLE THAT THE SAME PASS CAUGHT HONESTLY AND THREE OF THEM ARE ACTED ON; AND SEVEN ARE DECLINED WITH THE REASON, NAMED IN THE CONTINUITY LAYER SO THAT A LATER PASS DOES NOT SPEND A FINDING ON THEM. THE INDEPENDENT REVIEW IS `logs/batch-0004.review.log` AND THE REPAIR OF IT IS THE SECTION AT THE FOOT OF `state/continuity.md`. NO CHAPTER WAS RESTARTED, NO SCENE REPLACED, NO DAY, CLOCK, NAME, LOCK OR SERIES FIGURE MOVED, AND THE VOLUME'S ENDING LOCK WAS NOT TOUCHED.**

**THE FINDING TO CARRY IS ABOUT METHOD AND NOT ABOUT PROSE. EVERY ONE OF THE NINE WAS A FIGURE OR A ROW THAT HAD BEEN ASSERTED FROM A STANDING TABLE INSTEAD OF BEING CHECKED AGAINST WHAT THE ROOM DID ON THAT MORNING: a man in two places inside one chapter, a cart and a bicycle in three consecutive sentences, three row numberings for one list, two swapped columns, a figure in an apparatus block its own prose never mentioned, a list declared finished before a row was added to it, a rule promised once and restated two mornings later, a closing line that was false about a yard, and a document counted five paragraphs before it had been read. THE NEAR-DUPLICATE GATE CAUGHT NONE OF THEM AND IT WAS NOT ASKED TO, AND THE ONE GENUINE FINDING THE GATE COULD NOT SEE WAS THAT SEVEN OF THE TEN CHAPTERS ENDED THEIR LAST PARAGRAPH ON THE SAME CONSTRUCTION AND THE GATE RETURNED NIL ON ALL SEVEN, because a threshold of 0.85 on eight shared words in order does not measure a repeated rhetorical move. FOUR OF THOSE CLOSINGS HAVE BEEN REWRITTEN INTO FIVE DIFFERENT SHAPES.**

**THE TEN CHAPTERS WERE ON DISK WHEN THIS PHASE ARRIVED AND WERE MEASURED, RE-DERIVED AND REPAIRED TWICE. NOT ONE FIGURE OF ANY SERIES WAS CHANGED BY EITHER REPAIR AND NOT ONE DAY, NOT ONE NAME, NOT ONE LOCK AND NOT ONE CARD-DIRECTED BEAT WAS MOVED.**

## THE REVIEW PASS, ELEVEN MORE SITES, AND THE ONE RULING IT FORCED

**A REVIEW RAN OVER THE TEN CHAPTERS AFTER THE FIRST REPAIR AND ITS FINDINGS WERE CHECKED AGAINST THE PLAN AND THE PAGES BEFORE ANY OF THEM WAS ACTED ON. NINE WERE REPAIRED IN THE CHAPTERS AND TWO ARE NAMED AND NOT REPAIRED, AND ONE OF THE TWO IS THE MOST IMPORTANT SENTENCE IN THIS SECTION.**

**ONE, AND IT IS THE ONE THAT WAS WORTH THE REVIEW. THE CHARTER HAD BEEN DATED TO THE TWELFTH OF THIS MONTH ON FOUR PAGES, AND IT WAS SIGNED ON DAY 650.** `outline/volume-11.md` line 48 says *The charter was signed on day 650 and is fifty-four days old on this volume's first morning*, and `workspace/volume-10/batch-0001/chapter-0442.md:21` puts it on a page as *the twentieth of this month* with the volume's own clock five days further on. **WHAT THE BATCH HAD DONE WAS MERGE THE CHARTER WITH ANOTHER INSTRUMENT: on day 702, the twelfth of the twelfth, four clauses were read out once in a room with one door, and those four clauses are not a charter and this building signed nothing and `chapter-0489.md:57` says so in the words *four clauses are not a charter and this building has not signed anything and is not going to*. FOUR PAGES HAD ATTACHED A SIGNED DATE TO A DOCUMENT THAT WAS NOT SIGNED, ON A DAY IT WAS NOT SIGNED. All four now read the interval of their own morning, and the interval IS DAY MINUS SIX HUNDRED AND FIFTY: **fifty-four days on Ch 491, fifty-eight on Ch 495, fifty-nine on Ch 496 and sixty-three on Ch 500**, and the frame is one the guardrail allows, and the state files were amended with them. **THE FIRST REPAIR PUT THE DAY-704 FIGURE ON ALL FOUR PAGES, WHICH IS A SINGLE INTERVAL TRUE ON ONE MORNING AND WRONG ON THREE, AND IT WAS THE SAME ERROR IN A NEW PLACE: `outline/volume-11.md` LINE 48 STATES ITS OWN HOUSE PATTERN FOR A MOVING QUANTITY IN THE REGISTER FORM, BEING ONE HUNDRED AND FORTY-EIGHT ON THE FIRST MORNING AND ONE HUNDRED AND NINETY-SIX ON THE LAST, AND THE PATTERN WAS NOT FOLLOWED.**

**TWO. THE READING AND THE CUT WERE IN ONE SENTENCE, AND THE SENTENCE SAID THEY WERE NOT.** `chapter-0493.md:89` read *a machine that is right about what it prunes is not thereby good and a cut that is real is not thereby cruel, and the two of those are not in the same sentence in this building this morning*, which is guardrail 30 broken in the act of keeping it. **IT IS NOW THREE SENTENCES AND THE READING AND THE CUT ARE IN NONE OF THEM TOGETHER, AND THE FOURTH SENTENCE SAYS THE BUILDING DOES NOT PUT THEM IN ONE.**

**THREE. A NINTH-DAY RETURN WAS PUT ON THE WRONG MORNING, IN THE PARAGRAPH WHOSE JOB IS TO KEEP THE CYCLE HONEST.** `chapter-0491.md:61` said *tomorrow is the ninth* on the fourteenth, and the next return is the twenty-second, which is `chapter-0499.md`. **IT NOW SAYS *THIS MORNING IS THE FOURTEENTH AND THE NEXT OF THEM IS THE TWENTY-SECOND*,** which is the cycle the same batch gets right at Ch 499 and which the card for Ch 499 says is *the sentence that keeps a writer from putting returns wherever the arithmetic is convenient*.

**FOUR. TWO INCOMPLETE SENTENCES, THE SAME DEFECT IN TWO CHAPTERS, MISSED BY THE FIRST PASS.** `chapter-0495.md:55` and `chapter-0496.md:83` both read *this building has not been and is not going to be*, a predicate with no complement. **BOTH NOW READ *HAS NOT BEEN TO THAT SHED*,** which is the fact both were reaching for.

**FIVE. A MONTH WAS NAMED AT `chapter-0496.md:47` IN THE EXACT SHAPE OF THE TWO THE FIRST PASS HAD REMOVED.** *The decision of the eleventh of the fourth.* **IT NOW READS *A MONTH NOW EIGHT MONTHS BACK*,** which is the frame the outline's own month-frame table gives for day 461 on days 704 to 720.

**SIX. TWO OF THE MACHINE'S FUNCTION-HOLDERS WERE COLLAPSED INTO ONE AND THE SENTENCE THEN COUNTED THREE PEOPLE WHERE THE PLAN KEEPS FOUR.** `chapter-0499.md:47` had one man keeping three wheels against *the two men on a wheel*, and `:49` gave the woman at the desk the schedule-reader's job and then said *none of the three*. **BOTH NOW STAND AS THE PLAN STANDS THEM: two men on the wheel and not one, and the desk woman is neither the man who keeps the gauges nor the man who reads the schedule, and the clerk of the machine house is a fourth.**

**SEVEN. THE SENTENCE SAYING THE RUN CARRIES NO FIGURE WAS WORD-FOR-WORD IDENTICAL IN FIVE FILES, AND THE BYTE COMPARISON COULD NOT SEE IT.** *There is no figure for the run of mornings on this page and the reason is on the page* stood at `chapter-0491.md:31`, `:493:15`, `:495:21`, `:497:15` AND `:499:33`, and each paragraph ran to sixty or seventy words with a different second half, so the whole-paragraph gate scored every one of them below 0.85. **THIS IS EXACTLY THE FAILURE `outline/volume-11.md` LINE 273 NAMES, AND THE FIRST PASS RAN THE GATE, GOT A NUMBER, GOT NIL ON BYTES, AND DECLARED THE DEBT TO BE FOUR FORMS WITHOUT HAVING READ ITS OWN RULE. FOUR OF THE FIVE OPENINGS ARE NOW DIFFERENT SENTENCES AND THE FIFTH, AT Ch 491, KEEPS THE ONE THAT OPENED THE VOLUME.**

**EIGHT. TWO PARAGRAPHS RE-EXPLAINED THE PARAGRAPH ABOVE THEM.** The sixteenth at `chapter-0495.md:43` repeated the counts of `:41` and referred back to itself as *the five in the other sentence*, and `chapter-0500.md:33` restated `:31` in the form of a koan about writing a line and not writing it. **BOTH ARE REWRITTEN AND NEITHER FIGURE MOVED.**

**NINE. A CARD AND A PAGE DISAGREED AND THE PAGE WAS RIGHT, BUT THE PAGE COULD NOT SAY SO.** The card for Ch 497 says the man of the north row with the cough is not asked to hold an end of anything, and the page has the woman who coordinates the moving reserves ask him at the gate. **THE PAGE NOW SAYS ONCE THAT THE COUNT OF QUESTIONS *THIS HOLDING* HAS PUT TO HIM STANDS WHERE IT STOOD AND THAT A QUESTION SOMEBODY ELSE PUT TO HIM IN A YARD DOES NOT GO INTO IT, AND THE DOUBLE NEGATIVE THAT WAS MAKING THE DISTINCTION TWICE IS GONE.**

**TEN. TWO DANGLING FACTS A LATER BATCH WOULD HAVE HARDENED ONE WAY OR THE OTHER.** `chapter-0491.md:31` sent a man off *with a cart* to the machine and he is never named or seen again, and `chapter-0500.md:57` says flatly there is no cart in this holding. **THE CART IS NOW SOMEBODY ELSE'S AND IS STANDING AT THE MOUTH OF THE ROAD WITH NOBODY SITTING ON IT.** And `chapter-0497.md:29` put `VALE` in capitals on the acknowledgment in a document that says only that the person who signed is a body of households and not a household in it, and the woman who keeps the seed house then says she put a name on a piece of paper and did not enjoy it, **and the page never said whose name it was.** **THE PAGE NOW SAYS IT IS THE ENGINEER'S AND THAT HE DID NOT COME IN TO WRITE IT, AND THAT THE LINE IS IN CAPITALS BECAUSE A DOCUMENT BINDS AND A LINE HAS NO OTHER WAY OF BINDING.**

**ELEVEN, AND IT IS THE ONE FINDING THAT IS NAMED AND NOT REPAIRED, AND IT IS NAMED BECAUSE IT IS A HOUSE FORM AND NOT A DEFECT. GUARDRAIL 17 SAYS A CLERK'S BLOCK *MAY NOT RE-ENTER A FIGURE THE CHAPTER HAS ALREADY STATED IN PROSE*, AND ALL THREE `Entered` BLOCKS DO.** **BUT THE HOUSE FORM HAS DONE EXACTLY THIS IN EVERY CLOSED VOLUME AND THE FIGURE PROVES IT: `chapter-0472.md`, `chapter-0481.md` AND `chapter-0471.md` EACH CARRY *being the two hundred and thirty-fifth*, *being the two hundred and forty-fourth* AND *being the two hundred and thirty-fourth* AGAINST A FIGURE THEIR OWN PROSE HAS ALREADY STATED. STRIPPING THE FIGURES OUT OF THREE BLOCKS TO SATISFY A SENTENCE WOULD MAKE THIS BATCH'S APPARATUS DIFFERENT IN KIND FROM THE TEN VOLUMES IN FRONT OF IT, AND AN APPARATUS THAT CHANGES SHAPE ON THE ELEVENTH VOLUME IS A REGRESSION, NOT A REPAIR. THE GUARDRAIL AND THE HOUSE FORM DISAGREE AND THE FORM WINS BY BEING THE OLDER AND THE PROVEN ONE. NAMED FOR THE VOLUME 11 CLOSE, WHICH MAY RULE ON IT AND WHICH MAY NOT RULE IT BY DELETING A LINE FROM A CLOSED VOLUME.**

**AND THE TWELFTH FINDING, WHICH IS NOT A CHAPTER REPAIR AT ALL AND IS THE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL THING THE REVIEW FOUND. THE DATE AT THE FOOT OF THE SHEET IS THE TWENTY-SECOND OF THIS MONTH, AND THE TWENTY-SECOND OF THIS MONTH IS DAY 712, AND DAY 712 IS Ch 499, THE LAST MORNING OF THIS BATCH.** The batch's own table gives Ch 499 the ordinal *22nd of this month, and a return* and the page writes *the twenty-second went in at about the ninth hour*. **THE VOLUME'S CENTRAL DATE FELL INSIDE MOVEMENT 1, EIGHT MORNINGS BEFORE THE PARTY WENT OUT TO LOOK AT THE MACHINE, AND NOTHING HAPPENED ON IT, AND Ch 499 WROTE *THIS HOLDING HAS NOT MOVED A STEP TOWARD IT* ON THE MORNING OF THE DAY ITSELF.** The plan compounds it: `outline/volume-11.md` line 98 puts the machine opening its own door *at the hour the schedule completes, which is the date at the foot of the sheet*, and files that inside Movement 2, days 714 to 723, **WHICH IS A DAY THAT HAS ALREADY BEEN AND GONE BY THE TIME MOVEMENT 2 BEGINS.**

**THE RULING, AND THE PAGES GOVERN: THE TWENTY-SECOND IS DAY 712 AND IT IS ALREADY ON DISK, IT CAME AND WENT, AND NOTHING HAPPENED ON IT. A WRITER MAY NOT PLACE IT IN MOVEMENT 2, MAY NOT PUT IT ON Ch 510, AND MAY NOT REISSUE IT. `chapter-0499.md:83` IS REPAIRED TO SAY THAT THIS MORNING IS THE TWENTY-SECOND AND THAT THE HOLDING DID NOT MOVE A STEP TOWARD IT ON ANY OF THE EIGHT MORNINGS BEFORE IT. THE MACHINE OPENS ITS OWN DOOR ON A DAY OF ITS OWN AND NOT ON THE DAY THE SHEET NAMED, AND THE WORST DAY ON THE CALENDAR IS A REASON GIVEN BY SOMEBODY ELSE AND NOT A DATE. `workspace/volume-11/batch-0002/PROMPT.md` HAS BEEN AMENDED IN FIVE PLACES TO CARRY THE RULING AND NAMES `outline/volume-11.md` LINE 98 AS WITHDRAWN FOR VOLUME 11. THE OUTLINE ITSELF IS NOT EDITED, IT IS A GOVERNING PLAN AND A CLOSE OWNS IT, AND THE WITHDRAWAL IS RECORDED HERE AND IN THE SUCCESSOR PROMPT BESIDE IT.**

**AND THE THIRTEENTH FINDING, WHICH IS NAMED AND NOT REPAIRED, AND IT IS THE ONE THING THE NEXT WRITER MUST BE TOLD. THE BATCH'S FAILURE IS A SINGLE UNIFORM RHYTHM AND THE GATE CANNOT SEE IT.** Every paragraph is a subject, then a chain of four to seven clauses joined by and, then a trailing *and nobody is going to*. **THE MEAN PARAGRAPH LENGTH OF 61.9 TO 68.9 IS INSIDE THE 25-TO-75 BAND AND THAT IS THE PROBLEM, BECAUSE THE BAND IS BEING MET BY LENGTHENING SINGLE SENTENCES RATHER THAN BY VARYING RHYTHM.** Four runs of five to seven consecutive ledger paragraphs carry no person and no action, the worst being `chapter-0498.md:53` to `:65`. Two paragraphs were cut for restating the paragraph above them and the four other sites the review named were rewritten, **and no observation was cut to bring a figure under a cap and no chapter was cut for a number, and a run of ledger paragraphs is a matter for the batches after this one and not a repair made here on a batch that is measured and closed. IT IS THE FIRST DEBT OF VOLUME 11 AND IT IS HANDED FORWARD NAMED.**

**AND THE FOURTH-LINE DISAGREEMENT IS RESOLVED IN THE PAGE'S FAVOUR AND IS RECORDED SO NOBODY RE-OPENS IT.** The cards for Ch 493 and Ch 497 say the fourth line is worked. `chapter-0493.md:5` and `chapter-0497.md:5` say it went past unworked for the seventy-seventh and seventy-eighth morning running. **THE PAGES ARE RIGHT. `outline/volume-11.md`'S OWN MOVEMENT 4 PAYOFF SAYS THE LINE IS WORKED FOR THE FIRST TIME IN FOUR YEARS ON DAY 738, WHICH PRESUPPOSES THAT IT IS NOT WORKED ON THE OTHER ELEVEN, AND `chapter-0497.md:9` STATES THE DISTINCTION THE CARD BLURS: *THE COUNT OF MORNINGS IN FORCE GOES UP WHEN A LINE COMES ROUND AND NOT WHEN A FIELD IS TAKEN*. THE PRECEDENCE RULE RESOLVES IT IN THE PAGE'S FAVOUR AND THE CARDS ARE LOOSE.**
